85 /**
86 @ingroup transforms
87
88 @var for_each
89 @brief Callable object applying the Callable object f on each element of a product type.
90
91 `f` is applied on the values if the given product_type is a record type.
92
93 @note This function does not take part in overload resolution if `f` can't be applied to the
94 elements of `t` and/or `ts`, or if the product type are not compatible. @see compatible_product_types.
95
96 @qualifier inline
97 @qualifier constexpr
98
99 @groupheader{Header file}
100 @code
101 #include <kumi/algorithm/for_each.hpp>
102 @endcode
103
104 @groupheader{Call Signature}
105
106 @code
107 template<typename Function, product_type T, product_type... Ts>
108 constexpr decltype(auto) for_each(Function && f, T && t, Ts &&... ts);
109 @endcode
110
111 @subgroupheader{Parameters}
112
113 - `f`: Callable object to be invoked
114 - `t`: Product Type whose elements are used as arguments to f
115 - `ts`: Other product types whose elements are used as arguments to f
116
117 @see kumi::for_each_index
118 @see kumi::for_each_field
119
120 @groupheader{Examples}
121
122 @tab_begin
123
124 @tab{Tuple}
125 @godbolt{doc/tuple/algo/for_each.cpp}
126
127 @tab{Record}
128 @godbolt{doc/record/algo/for_each.cpp}
129
130 @tab_end
131 **/
132 //====================================================================================================================
133 inline constexpr for_each_t for_each{};
134

178 /**
